(ក). គណនា $\\\\lim_{x \\\\to +\\\\infty} f(x)$៖\\n$$\\\\begin{aligned} \\\\lim_{x \\\\to +\\\\infty} f(x) &= \\\\lim_{x \\\\to +\\\\infty} (4 - x - 2e^{-x}) \\\\\\\\ &= 4 - (+\\\\infty) - 2e^{-\\\\infty} \\\\\\\\ &= 4 - \\\\infty - 0 \\\\\\\\ &= -\\\\infty \\\\end{aligned}$$\\n\\n(ខ). បង្ហាញថាបន្ទាត់ $D: y = -x + 4$ ជាអាស៊ីមតូតទ្រេតនៃ $(C)$ ខាង $+\\\\infty$៖\\nយើងគណនាលីមីតនៃផលសង $f(x) - y$ កាលណា $x \\\\to +\\\\infty$៖\\n$$\\\\begin{aligned} \\\\lim_{x \\\\to +\\\\infty} [f(x) - (-x + 4)] &= \\\\lim_{x \\\\to +\\\\infty} (4 - x - 2e^{-x} + x - 4) \\\\\\\\ &= \\\\lim_{x \\\\to +\\\\infty} (-2e^{-x}) \\\\\\\\ &= -2(0) = 0 \\\\end{aligned}$$\\nដូចនេះ បន្ទាត់ $D: y = -x + 4$ ជាអាស៊ីមតូតទ្រេតនៃក្រាប $(C)$ ខាង $+\\\\infty$។\\n\\n(គ). ទីតាំងធៀបរវាងខ្សែរកោង $(C)$ និងបន្ទាត់ $(D)$៖\\nយើងសិក្សាសញ្ញានៃ $f(x) - y = -2e^{-x}$។\\nដោយសារ $e^{-x} > 0$ ចំពោះគ្រប់ $x \\\\in \\\\mathbb{R}$ នោះ $-2e^{-x} < 0$ ជានិច្ច។\\nដូចនេះ ខ្សែរកោង $(C)$ ស្ថិតនៅខាងក្រោមបន្ទាត់ $(D)$ ជានិច្ច។\\n\\n(ឃ). ផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់ថា $f(x) = \\\\frac{4e^x - xe^x - 2}{e^x}$ និងទាញរក $\\\\lim_{x \\\\to -\\\\infty} f(x)$៖\\n$$\\\\begin{aligned} f(x) &= 4 - x - 2e^{-x} \\\\\\\\ &= 4 - x - \\\\frac{2}{e^x} \\\\\\\\ &= \\\\frac{4e^x - xe^x - 2}{e^x} \\\\end{aligned}$$\\nគណនាលីមីតខាង $-\\\\infty$៖\\n$$\\\\begin{aligned} \\\\lim_{x \\\\to -\\\\infty} f(x) &= \\\\lim_{x \\\\to -\\\\infty} (4 - x - 2e^{-x}) \\\\\\\\ &= 4 - (-\\\\infty) - 2e^{-(-\\\\infty)} \\\\\\\\ &= 4 + \\\\infty - 2e^{+\\\\infty} \\\\\\\\ &= \\\\lim_{x \\\\to -\\\\infty} e^{-x} (4e^x - xe^x - 2) \\\\end{aligned}$$\\nដោយប្រើរាង $f(x) = 4 - x - 2e^{-x}$៖ កាលណា $x \\\\to -\\\\infty$ តួ $-2e^{-x} \\\\to -\\\\infty$ លឿនជាងតួ $-x \\\\to +\\\\infty$។\\n$$\\\\begin{aligned} \\\\lim_{x \\\\to -\\\\infty} f(x) &= \\\\lim_{x \\\\to -\\\\infty} e^{-x} (4e^x - xe^x - 2) \\\\\\\\ &= (+\\\\infty) \\\\times (0 - 0 - 2) \\\\\\\\ &= -\\\\infty \\\\end{aligned}$$\\n\\n២. (ក). គណនា $f'(x)$ និងសិក្សាអថេរភាព៖\\n$$\\\\begin{aligned} f'(x) &= (4 - x - 2e^{-x})' \\\\\\\\ &= -1 - 2(-e^{-x}) \\\\\\\\ &= -1 + 2e^{-x} \\\\end{aligned}$$\\nរកឫសនៃ $f'(x) = 0$៖\\n$$\\\\begin{aligned} -1 + 2e^{-x} &= 0 \\\\\\\\ 2e^{-x} &= 1 \\\\\\\\ e^{-x} &= \\\\frac{1}{2} \\\\\\\\ -x &= \\\\ln\\\\left(\\\\frac{1}{2}\\\\right) = -\\\\ln 2 \\\\\\\\ x &= \\\\ln 2 \\\\end{aligned}$$\\nតម្លៃអតិបរមា៖ $f(\\\\ln 2) = 4 - \\\\ln 2 - 2e^{-\\\\ln 2} = 4 - \\\\ln 2 - 2\\\\left(\\\\frac{1}{2}\\\\right) = 3 - \\\\ln 2 \\\\approx 2.31$។\\nតារាងអថេរភាព៖\\n$$\\\\begin{array}{|c|ccccc|} \\\\hline x & -\\\\infty & & \\\\ln 2 & & +\\\\infty \\\\\\\\ \\\\hline f'(x) & & + & 0 & - & \\\\\\\\ \\\\hline f(x) & & \\\\nearrow & 3-\\\\ln 2 & \\\\searrow & \\\\\\\\ & -\\\\infty & & & & -\\\\infty \\\\\\\\ \\\\hline \\\\end{array}$$\\n\\n(ខ). សមីការបន្ទាត់ប៉ះត្រង់ $x = 0$៖\\n$f(0) = 4 - 0 - 2e^0 = 4 - 2 = 2$ នាំឱ្យ $A(0, 2)$។\\n$f'(0) = -1 + 2e^0 = -1 + 2 = 1$។\\nសមីការបន្ទាត់ប៉ះ៖ $y = f'(0)(x - 0) + f(0) = 1(x) + 2 = x + 2$។\\n\\n(គ). បង្ហាញថាសមីការ $f(x) = 0$ មានចម្លើយតែមួយគត់ $\\\\beta \\\\in [-1, 0]$៖\\nលើចន្លោះ $(-\\\\infty, \\\\ln 2]$ អនុគមន៍ $f$ កើនដាច់ខាត និងជាប់។\\n$f(-1) = 4 - (-1) - 2e^1 = 5 - 2e \\\\approx 5 - 5.44 = -0.44 < 0$។\\n$f(0) = 2 > 0$។\\nដោយ $f(-1) \\\\times f(0) < 0$ តាមទ្រឹស្ដីបទតម្លៃកណ្ដាល សមីការ   "solutionText": "$f$ ជាអនុគមន៍កំណត់លើ $\\mathbb{R}$ ដោយ $f(x) = 4 - x - 2e^{-x}$ មានក្រាប $(C)$ ក្នុងតម្រុយអរតូណរម៉ាល់ $(O, \\vec{i}, \\vec{j})$។\n\n១. (ក). គណនា $\\lim_{x \\to +\\infty} f(x)$៖\n$$\\begin{aligned} \\lim_{x \\to +\\infty} f(x) &= \\lim_{x \\to +\\infty} (4 - x - 2e^{-x}) \\\\ &= 4 - (+\\infty) - 2e^{-\\infty} \\\\ &= 4 - \\infty - 0 \\\\ &= -\\infty \\end{aligned}$$\n\n(ខ). បង្ហាញថាបន្ទាត់ $D: y = -x + 4$ ជាអាស៊ីមតូតទ្រេតនៃ $(C)$ ខាង $+\\infty$៖\nយើងគណនាលីមីតនៃផលសង $f(x) - y$ កាលណា $x \\to +\\infty$៖\n$$\\begin{aligned} \\lim_{x \\to +\\infty} [f(x) - (-x + 4)] &= \\lim_{x \\to +\\infty} (4 - x - 2e^{-x} + x - 4) \\\\ &= \\lim_{x \\to +\\infty} (-2e^{-x}) \\\\ &= -2(0) = 0 \\end{aligned}$$\nដូចនេះ បន្ទាត់ $D: y = -x + 4$ ជាអាស៊ីមតូតទ្រេតនៃក្រាប $(C)$ ខាង $+\\infty$។\n\n(គ). ទីតាំងធៀបរវាងខ្សែរកោង $(C)$ និងបន្ទាត់ $(D)$៖\nយើងសិក្សាសញ្ញានៃ $f(x) - y = -2e^{-x}$។\nដោយសារ $e^{-x} > 0$ ចំពោះគ្រប់ $x \\in \\mathbb{R}$ នោះ $-2e^{-x} < 0$ ជានិច្ច។\nដូចនេះ ខ្សែរកោង $(C)$ ស្ថិតនៅខាងក្រោមបន្ទាត់ $(D)$ ជានិច្ច។\n\n(ឃ). ផ្ទៀងផ្ទាត់ថា $f(x) = \\frac{4e^x - xe^x - 2}{e^x}$ និងទាញរក $\\lim_{x \\to -\\infty} f(x)$៖\n$$\\begin{aligned} f(x) &= 4 - x - 2e^{-x} \\\\ &= 4 - x - \\frac{2}{e^x} \\\\ &= \\frac{4e^x - xe^x - 2}{e^x} \\end{aligned}$$\nគណនាលីមីតខាង $-\\infty$៖\n$$\\begin{aligned} \\lim_{x \\to -\\infty} f(x) &= \\lim_{x \\to -\\infty} (4 - x - 2e^{-x}) \\\\ &= 4 - (-\\infty) - 2e^{-(-\\infty)} \\\\ &= 4 + \\infty - 2e^{+\\infty} \\\\ &= \\lim_{x \\to -\\infty} e^{-x} (4e^x - xe^x - 2) \\end{aligned}$$\nដោយប្រើរាង $f(x) = 4 - x - 2e^{-x}$៖ កាលណា $x \\to -\\infty$ តួ $-2e^{-x} \\to -\\infty$ លឿនជាងតួ $-x \\to +\\infty$។\n$$\\begin{aligned} \\lim_{x \\to -\\infty} f(x) &= \\lim_{x \\to -\\infty} e^{-x} (4e^x - xe^x - 2) \\\\ &= (+\\infty) \\times (0 - 0 - 2) \\\\ &= -\\infty \\end{aligned}$$\n\n២. (ក). គណនា $f'(x)$ និងសិក្សាអថេរភាព៖\n$$\\begin{aligned} f'(x) &= (4 - x - 2e^{-x})' \\\\ &= -1 - 2(-e^{-x}) \\\\ &= -1 + 2e^{-x} \\end{aligned}$$\nរកឫសនៃ $f'(x) = 0$៖\n$$\\begin{aligned} -1 + 2e^{-x} &= 0 \\\\ 2e^{-x} &= 1 \\\\ e^{-x} &= \\frac{1}{2} \\\\ -x &= \\ln\\left(\\frac{1}{2}\\right) = -\\ln 2 \\\\ x &= \\ln 2 \\end{aligned}$$\nតម្លៃអតិបរមា៖ $f(\\ln 2) = 4 - \\ln 2 - 2e^{-\\ln 2} = 4 - \\ln 2 - 2\\left(\\frac{1}{2}\\right) = 3 - \\ln 2 \\approx 2.31$។\nតារាងអថេរភាព៖\n$$\\begin{array}{|c|ccccc|} \\hline x & -\\infty & & \\ln 2 & & +\\infty \\\\ \\hline f'(x) & & + & 0 & - & \\\\ \\hline f(x) & & \\nearrow & 3-\\ln 2 & \\searrow & \\\\ & -\\infty & & & & -\\infty \\\\ \\hline \\end{array}$$\n\n(ខ). សមីការបន្ទាត់ប៉ះត្រង់ $x = 0$៖\n$f(0) = 4 - 0 - 2e^0 = 4 - 2 = 2$ នាំឱ្យ $A(0, 2)$។\n$f'(0) = -1 + 2e^0 = -1 + 2 = 1$។\nសមីការបន្ទាត់ប៉ះ៖ $y = f'(0)(x - 0) + f(0) = 1(x) + 2 = x + 2$។\n\n(គ). បង្ហាញថាសមីការ $f(x) = 0$ មានចម្លើយតែមួយគត់ $\\beta \\in [-1, 0]$៖\nលើចន្លោះ $(-\\infty, \\ln 2]$ អនុគមន៍ $f$ កើនដាច់ខាត និងជាប់។\n$f(-1) = 4 - (-1) - 2e^1 = 5 - 2e \\approx 5 - 5.44 = -0.44 < 0$។\n$f(0) = 2 > 0$។\nដោយ $f(-1) \\times f(0) < 0$ តាមទ្រឹស្ដីបទតម្លៃកណ្ដាល សមីការ $f(x) = 0$ មានឫសតែមួយគត់ $\\beta \\in [-1, 0]$។",
